# Credits — เฮียผัด!

Third-party assets used in the game, with sources and licences as recorded by each research/feature agent.

> ⚠ Many images are real web photos of unknown licence (allowed for this private build at the client's request). Rows marked ⚠ or 'unknown'/'All Rights Reserved'/NC must be replaced or licensed before any public or commercial release. Kevin MacLeod's "Local Forecast - Elevator" (CC BY 4.0) must be credited in-game.


---

## R1-streets credits

Third-party sources of every file R1-streets shipped. Client rule change (2026-10-19): signs, plates, posters, facades and decals must come from real internet photos, any licence accepted, real brands allowed. Rows marked `unknown` are web images (Yandex/Flickr all-rights/stock-style sites) whose licence was NOT verified - the lead should review them before any public release. Everything else is CC0 / CC BY / CC BY-SA (attribution required for BY/BY-SA). Procedural ground materials list the CC0 scans they are built on.

## Credits — R2-food (labels, food textures, prop textures)

Generated by `scripts/research/R2/build_credits.py` from the three `manifest.json` files.

> **Licence warning.** On 2026-10-19 the client asked for real Thai product packaging and real food photos from the internet ("any licence"). Those files are marked *unknown*: they carry real trademarks and have not been cleared. They are fine for the private prototype, but before any public release they need permission or replacement (the parody label set and its generator are kept in `references/food/_raw/labels_parody_superseded/` and `scripts/research/R2/make_labels.py`). ambientCG assets are CC0.

---

## Credits — R3-people (faces, clothing, hair, ghosts)

Format: `shipped path | source URL (image ; page) | author | licence / terms`.

**Licence note.** Per the client's rule change ("any licence now OK; record source URLs"), many clothing/hair/ghost textures are derived from web photos whose licence is unknown (often stock/shop photos, all rights reserved by their owners). They are recorded below with image and page URLs. Before any public/commercial release, replace or license the rows marked *unknown*. Poly Haven scans and SFHQ synthetic faces are CC0.

Faces are synthetic people who do not exist (no real, identifiable person). Thai text in decals uses only looped Thai fonts (Sarabun, Chonburi; OFL, already in `public/assets/fonts/`).


## faces (`public/assets/textures/faces/`)

All face textures: derived from SFHQ (Synthetic Faces High Quality) by David Beniaguev — StyleGAN2 faces of people who do not exist. Licence CC0 1.0 (Kaggle dataset pages, parts 2 & 4); README: "no privacy issues or license issues… feel free to use as you see fit". Downloaded from the Hugging Face mirror https://huggingface.co/datasets/bitmind/SyntheticFacesHQ . Each face also ships `_detail.jpg` and `_mask.png` (same source); expression variants additionally use mouth interiors from SFHQ_pt2_00078890, SFHQ_pt4_00047693, SFHQ_pt4_00089594 (same dataset, CC0).

## R4-culture-ui — credits & licences

Format: **shipped path** (under `public/assets/img/`) | source URL | author | licence.

**Licence note (client rule change, Oct 2026):** the coordinator relayed the client's instruction to build the newspaper / magazine / posters from *real internet images, any licence*, recording source URLs. Several sources below are **All Rights Reserved** Flickr photos. They are heavily processed (halftoned, cropped, composited) but are **not licence-clean** — replace them with CC/PD equivalents (or obtain permission) before any public/commercial release. CC BY / CC BY-SA sources require attribution in the game credits; CC BY-SA derivatives (the composited pages) should be shared under CC BY-SA. Images marked CC BY-NC / NC-ND are non-commercial only.

## Own creations (no third-party content)
- `ui/*` (title, buttons, plates, arrow, labels) — rendered from OFL fonts (Chonburi, Noto Sans Thai Looped). OFL fonts are in `public/assets/fonts/`.
- All print simulation (halftone, misregistration, folds, stains, handwriting jitter, doodles), layouts, Thai copy and parody brands (ไทยรั่ว, ซอยเก๋, 8-Twelve, ตราเสือหลับ, น้องหมูดึ๋ง).
- `references/ui/album_cover_blocking.png`, `album_cover_palette.png` (diagram/swatches drawn by script; colours sampled from the client's reference).

## Look-only references (NOT shipped)
- `references/ui/web/newspaper/` — Wikimedia Commons: Thai Rath front pages 1968/1969 (public domain scans), 'Thai newspapers for sale at newsstand' (Maksym Kozlenko, CC BY-SA 4.0), 'Man in Tuk-Tuk with Newspaper' (Adam Jones, CC BY-SA 2.0). URLs in `_sources.tsv`.
- `references/ui/web/magazine/`, `references/ui/web/casino/` — Flickr, mostly All Rights Reserved; URLs in each `_sources.tsv`.
- `references/user/ref4_ALBUM_COVER_main_menu.jpg` — client-supplied (third-party album cover); only measured, never shipped.

## R6-pbr credits — PBR materials, foliage cards, HDRIs

All files below were produced by agent R6-pbr from the listed sources (scripts: `scripts/research/R6/`).
Processing: resizing, cropping, packing roughness/metalness/AO into ORM, colour/exposure correction, seamless
cross-fade or mirror tiling, cut-outs (alpha mattes) and normal maps derived from the photos/scans.
Files derived from **CC BY-SA** photos are themselves distributed under the same CC BY-SA licence;
**CC BY** photos require the attribution below. CC0 / Public-Domain-Mark sources need no attribution (credited anyway).

## Sculpted rigged bodies + animation library

| file | source | author | licence |
|---|---|---|---|
| body_m.glb, body_f.glb (+ .json metrics) | "Universal Base Characters [Standard]" — Superhero_Male_FullBody / Superhero_Female_FullBody (Godot-UE skeleton), https://quaternius.itch.io/universal-base-characters | Quaternius | CC0 |
| anims.glb | "Universal Animation Library [Standard]" — UAL1_Standard.glb (43 clips), https://quaternius.itch.io/universal-animation-library | Quaternius | CC0 |

Processing (headless Blender 4.2, `scripts/characters/build_chars.py`): seam-weld, muscle softening (less
"superhero"), eye sockets filled (static photo faces are painted on), eyes/brows/hair meshes removed, decimated to
4.6k / 1.5k tris, atlas-layout UVs, plane-cut clothing shells (tops/sleeves/bottoms/shoe) and skirt cones with
transferred weights, measured torso/neck/head tables for the procedural hair/accessory fitter. Clips are kept
unmodified and retargeted at load time (rest-pose delta per bone).

Clothing photo textures used through `PHOTO_USE` in atlas.ts (stripes, Hawaiian prints, ผ้าขาวม้า checks, denim,
suiting, satin/velvet, school twill, police khaki, vest/rider polys, ditsy blouses, apron, terry, sneaker leather) and
hair photos come from public/assets/textures/clothing|hair — sources/licences are listed in those manifests and in
references/credits/R3-people.md (research agent).

## intro module — credits for shipped files (`public/assets/gen/intro/`)

(processing script: kept in the intro agent's scratchpad, steps = crop, perspective-rectify, light-evening,
seamless offset blend, luminance keying). Audio comes from the shared manifest (see `R5-audio.md`).

## life module — credits (models, photos, sounds)

## Sounds

The life module uses the shared audio manifest (credits in `references/credits/R5-audio.md`): dog_bark, dog_whine, dog_eat, dog_happy_pant, dog_howl_night, cat_meow, cat_purr_loop, motorbike_pass, scooter_idle_loop, tuktuk_engine_loop, songthaew_engine_loop, car_horn_short, truck_horn, bus_airbrake, siren_distant, icecream_cart_bell, junk_buyer_loudspeaker, traffic_bangkok_loop, street_ambience_day/night_loop, market_crowd_loop, crowd_chatter_loop, birds_loop, cicadas_loop, crickets_frogs_night_loop, tv_muffled_loop, radio_distant_loop, tokay_gecko, rooster, temple_bell, crow, pigeons, eerie_drone_loop, ghost_scare_sting, ghost_laugh_female, whisper, wind_howl, motorcycle_ghost_engine_loop, crowd_laugh, female_scream_comedic, gasp, sparkle_chime, coin_clink, snore_loop. No extra audio files are shipped by life.

## Code-only (no third-party assets)

Noise-shader mist, glow and entrail shaders (`src/modules/life/fx.ts`), shell-texture fur (`creature.ts`), procedural fallback quadruped (`quad.ts`).

## social module — credits for shipped files (`public/assets/gen/social/`)

All art is cut from real photographs found online (client rule). Thai money: **only temple sides,
numerals and paper/guilloche patches are used — no portraits, no royal emblems** (brief §4).

| shipped file | what was done | source | author | licence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `money/coin10.jpg` | coin disc cropped (Wat Arun side of the bimetal 10 ฿), rim filled | https://www.flickr.com/photos/61697474@N00/481777658 (image https://live.staticflickr.com/201/481777658_7b769af0c1_b.jpg, via Openverse) | code_martial | CC BY-NC-SA 2.0 |
| `money/coin5.jpg` | coin disc cropped (Wat Benchamabophit side of the 5 ฿), rim filled | https://www.flickr.com/photos/78380111@N04/37177345432 | KingsIndian228 | CC BY-SA 2.0 |
| `money/note20.jpg`, `note50.jpg`, `note100.jpg` | big denomination numerals (rotated/levelled) + plain paper/guilloche patches of the real 20 / 50 / 100 ฿ notes, recomposed into a portrait-free note face | https://www.flickr.com/photos/50089567@N06/17011604479 ("Thai Bank Notes") | Karn B | CC BY-NC 2.0 |
| `handprint_red.png` | one white paint hand print isolated from a red wall (alpha from the paint), recoloured as a red slap welt | https://www.flickr.com/photos/28652129@N06/16035032225 ("Hands Down") | Anne Worner | CC BY-SA 2.0 |
| `sweat_drop.png` | a hanging water droplet cut out, desaturated & brightened | https://www.flickr.com/photos/77363333@N08/33177359694 ("Water Droplet Macro Biltmore Conservatory") | @CarShowShooter | CC BY-NC-SA 2.0 |
| `audio/crowd_ouch_1.mp3`, `audio/crowd_ouch_2.mp3` | two takes cut, faded, loudness-normalised | https://freesound.org/people/deleted_user_2104797/sounds/324897 ("Crowd Ouch.wav") | deleted_user_2104797 (Freesound) | CC0 1.0 |

Reused project assets (credited by their owners): `textures/props/cardboard_mat_*.jpg`,
`cardboard_corrugated_tile*.jpg` (real Mama / Chang carton prints, ambientCG Cardboard003),
`textures/food/basil_sprig_fresh.png`, `egg_fried_runny.png`, PBR `bamboo_poles`, `cardboard_worn`,
`stainless_brushed`, `cast_iron_wok`, `wood_teak`, `brass_gold`, `aluminium_scratched`, audio keys from
`public/assets/audio/manifest.json` (slap_hard, high_five, sad_trombone, level_up_fanfare, cloud_poof,
skill_tree_dreamy, sleep_lullaby, snore_loop, rooster, tug_strain, …). The thought cloud, sparkles,
glows and twinkles are procedural noise shaders (allowed for clouds / light effects).

## 3D text (`src/ui3d/text3d.ts`, `public/assets/gen/text3d/`)

- `public/assets/fonts/Krub-Bold.woff2` + `gen/text3d/talk.ttf` — **Krub** Bold, Copyright 2018 The Krub Project Authors (Cadson Demak, https://github.com/cadsondemak/Krub), SIL Open Font License 1.1, from https://github.com/google/fonts/tree/main/ofl/krub. Looped Thai (ไทยมีหัว). Default face of the floating 3D dialogue lettering.
- `gen/text3d/{heavy,ui,bubbly,display,hand,news}.ttf` — TTF subsets (Thai + Latin + punctuation) of the already-shipped OFL fonts K2D ExtraBold, IBM Plex Sans Thai Looped SemiBold, Kodchasan Bold, Chonburi, Itim and Taviraj Bold (SIL OFL 1.1, Google Fonts), converted with fontTools so HarfBuzz (`harfbuzzjs`, MIT) can shape them and extract outlines for extruded 3D letters.
